SELECT [t].[Id], [t].[CreatedAt], [t].[Description], [t].[DueDate], [t].[IsDeleted], [t].[Order], [t].[ProjectId], [t].[TaskStateId], [t].[Title], [t].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[Id], [p0].[CreatedAt], [p0].[Description], [p0].[IsDeleted], [p0].[Name], [p0].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[WorkspaceId], [t1].[Id], [t1].[CreatedAt], [t1].[IsDeleted], [t1].[Name], [t1].[Order], [t1].[UpdatedAt], [s].[Id], [s].[TagId], [s].[TaskItemId], [s].[Id0], [s].[Color], [s].[CreatedAt], [s].[IsDeleted], [s].[Name], [s].[UpdatedAt]
FROM [TaskItem] AS [t]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[IsDeleted], [p].[Name], [p].[UpdatedAt], [p].[WorkspaceId]
    FROM [Project] AS [p]
    WHERE [p].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [p0] ON [t].[ProjectId] = [p0].[Id]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [t0].[Id], [t0].[CreatedAt], [t0].[IsDeleted], [t0].[Name], [t0].[Order], [t0].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [TaskState] AS [t0]
    WHERE [t0].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t1] ON [t].[TaskStateId] = [t1].[Id]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [t2].[Id], [t2].[TagId], [t2].[TaskItemId], [t4].[Id] AS [Id0], [t4].[Color], [t4].[CreatedAt], [t4].[IsDeleted], [t4].[Name], [t4].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [TaskItemTag] AS [t2]
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT [t3].[Id], [t3].[Color], [t3].[CreatedAt], [t3].[IsDeleted], [t3].[Name], [t3].[UpdatedAt]
        FROM [Tag] AS [t3]
        WHERE [t3].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ) AS [t4] ON [t2].[TagId] = [t4].[Id]
) AS [s] ON [t].[Id] = [s].[TaskItemId]
WHERE [t].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
ORDER BY [t].[Id], [p0].[Id], [t1].[Id], [s].[Id]

SELECT [t].[Id], [t].[CreatedAt], [t].[Description], [t].[DueDate], [t].[IsDeleted], [t].[Order], [t].[ProjectId], [t].[TaskStateId], [t].[Title], [t].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[Id], [p0].[CreatedAt], [p0].[Description], [p0].[IsDeleted], [p0].[Name], [p0].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[WorkspaceId], [t1].[Id], [t1].[CreatedAt], [t1].[IsDeleted], [t1].[Name], [t1].[Order], [t1].[UpdatedAt], [s].[Id], [s].[TagId], [s].[TaskItemId], [s].[Id0], [s].[Color], [s].[CreatedAt], [s].[IsDeleted], [s].[Name], [s].[UpdatedAt]
FROM [TaskItem] AS [t]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[IsDeleted], [p].[Name], [p].[UpdatedAt], [p].[WorkspaceId]
    FROM [Project] AS [p]
    WHERE [p].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [p0] ON [t].[ProjectId] = [p0].[Id]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [t0].[Id], [t0].[CreatedAt], [t0].[IsDeleted], [t0].[Name], [t0].[Order], [t0].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [TaskState] AS [t0]
    WHERE [t0].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t1] ON [t].[TaskStateId] = [t1].[Id]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [t2].[Id], [t2].[TagId], [t2].[TaskItemId], [t4].[Id] AS [Id0], [t4].[Color], [t4].[CreatedAt], [t4].[IsDeleted], [t4].[Name], [t4].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [TaskItemTag] AS [t2]
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT [t3].[Id], [t3].[Color], [t3].[CreatedAt], [t3].[IsDeleted], [t3].[Name], [t3].[UpdatedAt]
        FROM [Tag] AS [t3]
        WHERE [t3].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ) AS [t4] ON [t2].[TagId] = [t4].[Id]
) AS [s] ON [t].[Id] = [s].[TaskItemId]
WHERE [t].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
ORDER BY [t].[Id], [p0].[Id], [t1].[Id], [s].[Id]

SELECT [t].[Id], [t].[CreatedAt], [t].[Description], [t].[DueDate], [t].[IsDeleted], [t].[Order], [t].[ProjectId], [t].[TaskStateId], [t].[Title], [t].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[Id], [p0].[CreatedAt], [p0].[Description], [p0].[IsDeleted], [p0].[Name], [p0].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[WorkspaceId], [t1].[Id], [t1].[CreatedAt], [t1].[IsDeleted], [t1].[Name], [t1].[Order], [t1].[UpdatedAt], [s].[Id], [s].[TagId], [s].[TaskItemId], [s].[Id0], [s].[Color], [s].[CreatedAt], [s].[IsDeleted], [s].[Name], [s].[UpdatedAt]
FROM [TaskItem] AS [t]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[IsDeleted], [p].[Name], [p].[UpdatedAt], [p].[WorkspaceId]
    FROM [Project] AS [p]
    WHERE [p].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [p0] ON [t].[ProjectId] = [p0].[Id]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [t0].[Id], [t0].[CreatedAt], [t0].[IsDeleted], [t0].[Name], [t0].[Order], [t0].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [TaskState] AS [t0]
    WHERE [t0].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t1] ON [t].[TaskStateId] = [t1].[Id]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [t2].[Id], [t2].[TagId], [t2].[TaskItemId], [t4].[Id] AS [Id0], [t4].[Color], [t4].[CreatedAt], [t4].[IsDeleted], [t4].[Name], [t4].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [TaskItemTag] AS [t2]
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT [t3].[Id], [t3].[Color], [t3].[CreatedAt], [t3].[IsDeleted], [t3].[Name], [t3].[UpdatedAt]
        FROM [Tag] AS [t3]
        WHERE [t3].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ) AS [t4] ON [t2].[TagId] = [t4].[Id]
) AS [s] ON [t].[Id] = [s].[TaskItemId]
WHERE [t].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
ORDER BY [t].[Id], [p0].[Id], [t1].[Id], [s].[Id]

2026-07-28 09:48:53.931 +03:30 [INF] Executed DbCommand (189ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT [t].[Id], [t].[CreatedAt], [t].[Description], [t].[DueDate], [t].[IsDeleted], [t].[Order], [t].[ProjectId], [t].[TaskStateId], [t].[Title], [t].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[Id], [p0].[CreatedAt], [p0].[Description], [p0].[IsDeleted], [p0].[Name], [p0].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[WorkspaceId], [t1].[Id], [t1].[CreatedAt], [t1].[IsDeleted], [t1].[Name], [t1].[Order], [t1].[UpdatedAt], [s].[Id], [s].[TagId], [s].[TaskId], [s].[Id0], [s].[Color], [s].[CreatedAt], [s].[IsDeleted], [s].[Name], [s].[UpdatedAt]
FROM [Task] AS [t]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[IsDeleted], [p].[Name], [p].[UpdatedAt], [p].[WorkspaceId]
    FROM [Project] AS [p]
    WHERE [p].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [p0] ON [t].[ProjectId] = [p0].[Id]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [t0].[Id], [t0].[CreatedAt], [t0].[IsDeleted], [t0].[Name], [t0].[Order], [t0].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [TaskState] AS [t0]
    WHERE [t0].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t1] ON [t].[TaskStateId] = [t1].[Id]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [t2].[Id], [t2].[TagId], [t2].[TaskId], [t4].[Id] AS [Id0], [t4].[Color], [t4].[CreatedAt], [t4].[IsDeleted], [t4].[Name], [t4].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [TaskTag] AS [t2]
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT [t3].[Id], [t3].[Color], [t3].[CreatedAt], [t3].[IsDeleted], [t3].[Name], [t3].[UpdatedAt]
        FROM [Tag] AS [t3]
        WHERE [t3].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ) AS [t4] ON [t2].[TagId] = [t4].[Id]
) AS [s] ON [t].[Id] = [s].[TaskId]
WHERE [t].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
ORDER BY [t].[Id], [p0].[Id], [t1].[Id], [s].[Id]

FROM [Task] AS [t]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[IsDeleted], [p].[Name], [p].[UpdatedAt], [p].[WorkspaceId]
    FROM [Project] AS [p]
    WHERE [p].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [p0] ON [t].[ProjectId] = [p0].[Id]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [t0].[Id], [t0].[CreatedAt], [t0].[IsDeleted], [t0].[Name], [t0].[Order], [t0].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [TaskState] AS [t0]
    WHERE [t0].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t1] ON [t].[TaskStateId] = [t1].[Id]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [t2].[Id], [t2].[TagId], [t2].[TaskId], [t4].[Id] AS [Id0], [t4].[Color], [t4].[CreatedAt], [t4].[IsDeleted], [t4].[Name], [t4].[UpdatedAt]

        SELECT [t3].[Id], [t3].[Color], [t3].[CreatedAt], [t3].[IsDeleted], [t3].[Name], [t3].[UpdatedAt]
        FROM [Tag] AS [t3]
        WHERE [t3].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ) AS [t4] ON [t2].[TagId] = [t4].[Id]
) AS [s] ON [t].[Id] = [s].[TaskId]
WHERE [t].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
ORDER BY [t].[Id], [p0].[Id], [t1].[Id], [s].[Id]

SELECT [t].[Id], [t].[CreatedAt], [t].[Description], [t].[DueDate], [t].[IsDeleted], [t].[Order], [t].[ProjectId], [t].[TaskStateId], [t].[Title], [t].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[Id], [p0].[CreatedAt], [p0].[Description], [p0].[IsDeleted], [p0].[Name], [p0].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[WorkspaceId], [t1].[Id], [t1].[CreatedAt], [t1].[IsDeleted], [t1].[Name], [t1].[Order], [t1].[UpdatedAt], [s].[Id], [s].[TagId], [s].[TaskId], [s].[Id0], [s].[Color], [s].[CreatedAt], [s].[IsDeleted], [s].[Name], [s].[UpdatedAt]
FROM [Task] AS [t]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[IsDeleted], [p].[Name], [p].[UpdatedAt], [p].[WorkspaceId]
    FROM [Project] AS [p]
    WHERE [p].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [p0] ON [t].[ProjectId] = [p0].[Id]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [t0].[Id], [t0].[CreatedAt], [t0].[IsDeleted], [t0].[Name], [t0].[Order], [t0].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [TaskState] AS [t0]
    WHERE [t0].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t1] ON [t].[TaskStateId] = [t1].[Id]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [t2].[Id], [t2].[TagId], [t2].[TaskId], [t4].[Id] AS [Id0], [t4].[Color], [t4].[CreatedAt], [t4].[IsDeleted], [t4].[Name], [t4].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [TaskTag] AS [t2]
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT [t3].[Id], [t3].[Color], [t3].[CreatedAt], [t3].[IsDeleted], [t3].[Name], [t3].[UpdatedAt]
        FROM [Tag] AS [t3]
        WHERE [t3].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ) AS [t4] ON [t2].[TagId] = [t4].[Id]
) AS [s] ON [t].[Id] = [s].[TaskId]
WHERE [t].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
ORDER BY [t].[Id], [p0].[Id], [t1].[Id], [s].[Id]

SELECT [t].[Id], [t].[CreatedAt], [t].[Description], [t].[DueDate], [t].[IsDeleted], [t].[Order], [t].[ProjectId], [t].[TaskStateId], [t].[Title], [t].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[Id], [p0].[CreatedAt], [p0].[Description], [p0].[IsDeleted], [p0].[Name], [p0].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[WorkspaceId], [t1].[Id], [t1].[CreatedAt], [t1].[IsDeleted], [t1].[Name], [t1].[Order], [t1].[UpdatedAt], [s].[Id], [s].[TagId], [s].[TaskId], [s].[Id0], [s].[Color], [s].[CreatedAt], [s].[IsDeleted], [s].[Name], [s].[UpdatedAt]
FROM [Task] AS [t]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[IsDeleted], [p].[Name], [p].[UpdatedAt], [p].[WorkspaceId]
    FROM [Project] AS [p]
    WHERE [p].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [p0] ON [t].[ProjectId] = [p0].[Id]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [t0].[Id], [t0].[CreatedAt], [t0].[IsDeleted], [t0].[Name], [t0].[Order], [t0].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [TaskState] AS [t0]
    WHERE [t0].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t1] ON [t].[TaskStateId] = [t1].[Id]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [t2].[Id], [t2].[TagId], [t2].[TaskId], [t4].[Id] AS [Id0], [t4].[Color], [t4].[CreatedAt], [t4].[IsDeleted], [t4].[Name], [t4].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [TaskTag] AS [t2]
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT [t3].[Id], [t3].[Color], [t3].[CreatedAt], [t3].[IsDeleted], [t3].[Name], [t3].[UpdatedAt]
        FROM [Tag] AS [t3]
        WHERE [t3].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ) AS [t4] ON [t2].[TagId] = [t4].[Id]
) AS [s] ON [t].[Id] = [s].[TaskId]
WHERE [t].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
ORDER BY [t].[Id], [p0].[Id], [t1].[Id], [s].[Id]

SELECT [t].[Id], [t].[CreatedAt], [t].[Description], [t].[DueDate], [t].[IsDeleted], [t].[Order], [t].[ProjectId], [t].[TaskStateId], [t].[Title], [t].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[Id], [p0].[CreatedAt], [p0].[Description], [p0].[IsDeleted], [p0].[Name], [p0].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[WorkspaceId], [t1].[Id], [t1].[CreatedAt], [t1].[IsDeleted], [t1].[Name], [t1].[Order], [t1].[UpdatedAt], [s].[Id], [s].[TagId], [s].[TaskId], [s].[Id0], [s].[Color], [s].[CreatedAt], [s].[IsDeleted], [s].[Name], [s].[UpdatedAt]
FROM [Task] AS [t]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[IsDeleted], [p].[Name], [p].[UpdatedAt], [p].[WorkspaceId]
    FROM [Project] AS [p]
    WHERE [p].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [p0] ON [t].[ProjectId] = [p0].[Id]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [t0].[Id], [t0].[CreatedAt], [t0].[IsDeleted], [t0].[Name], [t0].[Order], [t0].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [TaskState] AS [t0]
    WHERE [t0].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t1] ON [t].[TaskStateId] = [t1].[Id]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [t2].[Id], [t2].[TagId], [t2].[TaskId], [t4].[Id] AS [Id0], [t4].[Color], [t4].[CreatedAt], [t4].[IsDeleted], [t4].[Name], [t4].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [TaskTag] AS [t2]
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT [t3].[Id], [t3].[Color], [t3].[CreatedAt], [t3].[IsDeleted], [t3].[Name], [t3].[UpdatedAt]
        FROM [Tag] AS [t3]
        WHERE [t3].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ) AS [t4] ON [t2].[TagId] = [t4].[Id]
) AS [s] ON [t].[Id] = [s].[TaskId]
WHERE [t].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
ORDER BY [t].[Id], [p0].[Id], [t1].[Id], [s].[Id]

2026-07-28 09:58:55.597 +03:30 [INF] Executed DbCommand (37ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT [t].[Id], [t].[CreatedAt], [t].[Description], [t].[DueDate], [t].[IsDeleted], [t].[Order], [t].[ProjectId], [t].[TaskStateId], [t].[Title], [t].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[Id], [p0].[CreatedAt], [p0].[Description], [p0].[IsDeleted], [p0].[Name], [p0].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[WorkspaceId], [t1].[Id], [t1].[CreatedAt], [t1].[IsDeleted], [t1].[Name], [t1].[Order], [t1].[UpdatedAt], [s].[Id], [s].[TagId], [s].[TaskId], [s].[Id0], [s].[Color], [s].[CreatedAt], [s].[IsDeleted], [s].[Name], [s].[UpdatedAt]
FROM [Task] AS [t]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[IsDeleted], [p].[Name], [p].[UpdatedAt], [p].[WorkspaceId]
    FROM [Project] AS [p]
    WHERE [p].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [p0] ON [t].[ProjectId] = [p0].[Id]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [t0].[Id], [t0].[CreatedAt], [t0].[IsDeleted], [t0].[Name], [t0].[Order], [t0].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [TaskState] AS [t0]
    WHERE [t0].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t1] ON [t].[TaskStateId] = [t1].[Id]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [t2].[Id], [t2].[TagId], [t2].[TaskId], [t4].[Id] AS [Id0], [t4].[Color], [t4].[CreatedAt], [t4].[IsDeleted], [t4].[Name], [t4].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [TaskTag] AS [t2]
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT [t3].[Id], [t3].[Color], [t3].[CreatedAt], [t3].[IsDeleted], [t3].[Name], [t3].[UpdatedAt]
        FROM [Tag] AS [t3]
        WHERE [t3].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ) AS [t4] ON [t2].[TagId] = [t4].[Id]
) AS [s] ON [t].[Id] = [s].[TaskId]
WHERE [t].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
